##### P01, version A — Trevor Eden, 42 in 2036
  1  2031: Greta Vaughan moved to the town for a job at the school, with Trevor Eden.  [the school had been short-staffed for 9 months]
  2  2031: Trevor Eden started work at the sawmill.  [the only one looking for work; nobody better had applied for months]
  3  2031: Trevor Eden tells the story of every one of his own disasters better than anyone, and always against himself.  [that sense of humour]

  6  2032: Trevor Eden says there's nothing for him here.  [nothing holding them here; wanted to know]
  7  2033: Greta Vaughan went at Trevor Eden about his family, and it got personal fast.  [it had been building between them (his family)]
  8  2033: Trevor Eden stopped answering and stared at the television until Greta Vaughan gave up.  [Greta Vaughan went at Trevor Eden about his family, and it got personal fast.; has never been one to look back]
  9  2034: Trevor Eden went to pieces during the visit from head office at the sawmill.  [could not stop thinking about what might go wrong; dreads being looked at]
 10  2034: At the sawmill, Trevor Eden goes quiet and tense before big moments.  [could not stop thinking about what might go wrong]

       ↳ 2033: Neil was found out: Ken had been blamed for Neil's mistake.
 12  2035: Greta Vaughan had begun to wonder whether to stay with Trevor Eden.  [things had not been good with Trevor Eden for a while]

       ↳ 2035: Greta Vaughan let how little time they had together go again.

 15  2036: Trevor Eden still holds Greta Vaughan's hand on the way to the quay.  [shows it in what he does; together since 2027]
